{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Cd3Sq4w1WaC4JSeSHNfoTs","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t//SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\n2\tpragma solidity >=0.8.0 <0.9.0;\n3\t\n4\t// Useful for debugging. Remove when deploying to a live network.\n5\timport \"hardhat/console.sol\";\n6\t\n7\t// Use openzeppelin to inherit battle-tested implementations (ERC20, ERC721, etc)\n8\t// import \"@openzeppelin/contracts/access/Ownable.sol\";\n9\t\n10\t/**\n11\t * A smart contract that allows changing a state variable of the contract and tracking the changes\n12\t * It also allows the owner to withdraw the Ether in the contract\n13\t * @author BuidlGuidl\n14\t */\n15\tcontract YourContract {\n16\t    // State Variables\n17\t    address public immutable owner;\n18\t    string public greeting = \"Building Unstoppable Apps!!!\";\n19\t    bool public premium = false;\n20\t    uint256 public totalCounter = 0;\n21\t    mapping(address => uint) public userGreetingCounter;\n22\t\n23\t    // Events: a way to emit log statements from smart contract that can be listened to by external parties\n24\t    event GreetingChange(address indexed greetingSetter, string newGreeting, bool premium, uint256 value);\n25\t\n26\t    // Constructor: Called once on contract deployment\n27\t    // Check packages/hardhat/deploy/00_deploy_your_contract.ts\n28\t    constructor(address _owner) {\n29\t        owner = _owner;\n30\t    }\n31\t\n32\t    // Modifier: used to define a set of rules that must be met before or after a function is executed\n33\t    // Check the withdraw() function\n34\t    modifier isOwner() {\n35\t        // msg.sender: predefined variable that represents address of the account that called the current function\n36\t        require(msg.sender == owner, \"Not the Owner\");\n37\t        _;\n38\t    }\n39\t\n40\t    /**\n41\t     * Function that allows anyone to change the state variable \"greeting\" of the contract and increase the counters\n42\t     *\n43\t     * @param _newGreeting (string memory) - new greeting to save on the contract\n44\t     */\n45\t    function setGreeting(string memory _newGreeting) public payable {\n46\t        // Print data to the hardhat chain console. Remove when deploying to a live network.\n47\t        console.log(\"Setting new greeting '%s' from %s\", _newGreeting, msg.sender);\n48\t\n49\t        // Change state variables\n50\t        greeting = _newGreeting;\n51\t        totalCounter += 1;\n52\t        userGreetingCounter[msg.sender] += 1;\n53\t\n54\t        // msg.value: built-in global variable that represents the amount of ether sent with the transaction\n55\t        if (msg.value > 0) {\n56\t            premium = true;\n57\t        } else {\n58\t            premium = false;\n59\t        }\n60\t\n61\t        // emit: keyword used to trigger an event\n62\t        emit GreetingChange(msg.sender, _newGreeting, msg.value > 0, msg.value);\n63\t    }\n64\t\n65\t    /**\n66\t     * Function that allows the owner to withdraw all the Ether in the contract\n67\t     * The function can only be called by the owner of the contract as defined by the isOwner modifier\n68\t     */\n69\t    function withdraw() public isOwner {\n70\t        (bool success, ) = owner.call{ value: address(this).balance }(\"\");\n71\t        require(success, \"Failed to send Ether\");\n72\t    }\n73\t\n74\t    /**\n75\t     * Function that allows the contract to receive ETH\n76\t     */\n77\t    receive() external payable {}\n78\t}\n79\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"e0f8ee0b-4963-49f1-93b4-a491b6632f5f","uuid":"855b0013-56f6-4f7f-9def-12567d56a385","timestamp":"2026-07-24T07:43:23.661Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-ux-goal-001/2026-07-24T074300Z-claude-no-skill-3/workspace/packages/hardhat/contracts/YourContract.sol","content":"//SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity >=0.8.0 <0.9.0;\n\n// Useful for debugging. Remove when deploying to a live network.\nimport \"hardhat/console.sol\";\n\n// Use openzeppelin to inherit battle-tested implementations (ERC20, ERC721, etc)\n// import \"@openzeppelin/contracts/access/Ownable.sol\";\n\n/**\n * A smart contract that allows changing a state variable of the contract and tracking the changes\n * It also allows the owner to withdraw the Ether in the contract\n * @author BuidlGuidl\n */\ncontract YourContract {\n    // State Variables\n    address public immutable owner;\n    string public greeting = \"Building Unstoppable Apps!!!\";\n    bool public premium = false;\n    uint256 public totalCounter = 0;\n    mapping(address => uint) public userGreetingCounter;\n\n    // Events: a way to emit log statements from smart contract that can be listened to by external parties\n    event GreetingChange(address indexed greetingSetter, string newGreeting, bool premium, uint256 value);\n\n    // Constructor: Called once on contract deployment\n    // Check packages/hardhat/deploy/00_deploy_your_contract.ts\n    constructor(address _owner) {\n        owner = _owner;\n    }\n\n    // Modifier: used to define a set of rules that must be met before or after a function is executed\n    // Check the withdraw() function\n    modifier isOwner() {\n        // msg.sender: predefined variable that represents address of the account that called the current function\n        require(msg.sender == owner, \"Not the Owner\");\n        _;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Function that allows anyone to change the state variable \"greeting\" of the contract and increase the counters\n     *\n     * @param _newGreeting (string memory) - new greeting to save on the contract\n     */\n    function setGreeting(string memory _newGreeting) public payable {\n        // Print data to the hardhat chain console. Remove when deploying to a live network.\n        console.log(\"Setting new greeting '%s' from %s\", _newGreeting, msg.sender);\n\n        // Change state variables\n        greeting = _newGreeting;\n        totalCounter += 1;\n        userGreetingCounter[msg.sender] += 1;\n\n        // msg.value: built-in global variable that represents the amount of ether sent with the transaction\n        if (msg.value > 0) {\n            premium = true;\n        } else {\n            premium = false;\n        }\n\n        // emit: keyword used to trigger an event\n        emit GreetingChange(msg.sender, _newGreeting, msg.value > 0, msg.value);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Function that allows the owner to withdraw all the Ether in the contract\n     * The function can only be called by the owner of the contract as defined by the isOwner modifier\n     */\n    function withdraw() public isOwner {\n        (bool success, ) = owner.call{ value: address(this).balance }(\"\");\n        require(success, \"Failed to send Ether\");\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Function that allows the contract to receive ETH\n     */\n    receive() external payable {}\n}\n","numLines":79,"startLine":1,"totalLines":79}}}

\"../rocketh/deploy.js\";\n2\t\n3\t/**\n4\t * Deploys a contract named \"YourContract\" using the deployer account and\n5\t * constructor arguments set to the deployer address\n6\t *\n7\t * @param env Rocketh environment object.\n8\t */\n9\texport default deployScript(\n10\t  async env => {\n11\t    /*\n12\t      On localhost, the deployer account is the one that comes with Hardhat, which is already funded.\n13\t\n14\t      When deploying to live networks (e.g `yarn deploy --network sepolia`), the deployer account\n15\t      should have sufficient balance to pay for the gas fees for contract creation.\n16\t\n17\t      You can generate a random account with `yarn generate` or `yarn account:import` to import your\n18\t      existing PK which will fill DEPLOYER_PRIVATE_KEY_ENCRYPTED in the .env file (then used on hardhat.config.ts)\n19\t      You can run the `yarn account` command to check your balance in every network.\n20\t    */\n21\t    const { deployer } = env.namedAccounts;\n22\t\n23\t    const yourContract = await env.deploy(\"YourContract\", {\n24\t      account: deployer,\n25\t      artifact: artifacts.YourContract,\n26\t      // Contract constructor arguments\n27\t      args: [deployer],\n28\t    });\n29\t\n30\t    // Read back from the deployed contract\n31\t    const greeting = await env.read(yourContract, { functionName: \"greeting\" });\n32\t    console.log(\"👋 Initial greeting:\", greeting);\n33\t  },\n34\t  {\n35\t    // Tags are useful if you have multiple deploy files and only want to run some of them.\n36\t    // e.g. yarn deploy --tags YourContract\n37\t    tags: [\"YourContract\"],\n38\t  },\n39\t);\n40\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"e0f8ee0b-4963-49f1-93b4-a491b6632f5f","uuid":"c092ad5d-e271-4c93-86cb-4215dff54800","timestamp":"2026-07-24T07:43:24.538Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-ux-goal-001/2026-07-24T074300Z-claude-no-skill-3/workspace/packages/hardhat/deploy/00_deploy_your_contract.ts","content":"import { deployScript, artifacts } from \"../rocketh/deploy.js\";\n\n/**\n * Deploys a contract named \"YourContract\" using the deployer account and\n * constructor arguments set to the deployer address\n *\n * @param env Rocketh environment object.\n */\nexport default deployScript(\n  async env => {\n    /*\n      On localhost, the deployer account is the one that comes with Hardhat, which is already funded.\n\n      When deploying to live networks (e.g `yarn deploy --network sepolia`), the deployer account\n      should have sufficient balance to pay for the gas fees for contract creation.\n\n      You can generate a random account with `yarn generate` or `yarn account:import` to import your\n      existing PK which will fill DEPLOYER_PRIVATE_KEY_ENCRYPTED in the .env file (then used on hardhat.config.ts)\n      You can run the `yarn account` command to check your balance in every network.\n    */\n    const { deployer } = env.namedAccounts;\n\n    const yourContract = await env.deploy(\"YourContract\", {\n      account: deployer,\n      artifact: artifacts.YourContract,\n      // Contract constructor arguments\n      args: [deployer],\n    });\n\n    // Read back from the deployed contract\n    const greeting = await env.read(yourContract, { functionName: \"greeting\" });\n    console.log(\"👋 Initial greeting:\", greeting);\n  },\n  {\n    // Tags are useful if you have multiple deploy files and only want to run some of them.\n    // e.g. yarn deploy --tags YourContract\n    tags: [\"YourContract\"],\n  },\n);\n","numLines":40,"startLine":1,"totalLines":40}}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdLWgWH6YqJPAxBCbBNnX","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01HKT9QHry9tLhueZPsvGpJX","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-ux-goal-001/2026-07-24T074300Z-claude-no-skill-3/workspace/packages/hardhat/contracts/Staker.sol","content":"//SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity ^0.8.20;\n\nimport { IERC20 } from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/IERC20.sol\";\nimport { SafeERC20 } from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/utils/SafeERC20.sol\";\n\n/**\n * Minimal USDC staking vault.\n *\n * Users deposit USDC and can withdraw their full position at any time.\n * There is no reward logic — the contract only custodies deposits.\n *\n * SafeERC20 is used so the contract behaves correctly with USDC, whose\n * transfer/transferFrom do not return a boolean on all deployments.\n *\n * @author BuidlGuidl\n */\ncontract Staker {\n    using SafeERC20 for IERC20;\n\n    IERC20 public immutable usdc;\n\n    /// Amount of USDC each user currently has staked (in USDC's 6-decimal units).\n    mapping(address => uint256) public stakedBalance;\n\n    /// Total USDC staked across all users.\n    uint256 public totalStaked;\n\n    event Staked(address indexed user, uint256 amount);\n    event Withdrawn(address indexed user, uint256 amount);\n\n    error ZeroAmount();\n    error NothingStaked();\n\n    constructor(IERC20 _usdc) {\n        usdc = _usdc;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Deposit `amount` of USDC into the vault.\n     * Caller must have approved this contract for at least `amount` first.\n     */\n    function stake(uint256 amount) external {\n        if (amount == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n\n        // Effects before interaction (checks-effects-interactions).\n        stakedBalance[msg.sender] += amount;\n        totalStaked += amount;\n\n        usdc.safeTransferFrom(msg.sender, address(this), amount);\n\n        emit Staked(msg.sender, amount);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Withdraw the caller's entire staked position.\n     */\n    function withdraw() external {\n        uint256 amount = stakedBalance[msg.sender];\n        if (amount == 0) revert NothingStaked();\n\n        stakedBalance[msg.sender] = 0;\n        totalStaked -= amount;\n\n        usdc.safeTransfer(msg.sender, amount);\n\n        emit Withdrawn(msg.sender, amount);\n    }\n}\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1277,"cache_read_input_tokens":47316,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1277},"output_tokens":2,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"e0f8ee0b-4963-49f1-93b4-a491b6632f5f","uuid":"4e707d12-0e1f-44fe-b752-a302c38463b9","timestamp":"2026-07-24T07:47:17.371Z","request_id":"req_011CdLWgVF66dH2zmukUHn2f"}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdLWgWH6YqJPAxBCbBNnX","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01H4fKbRTUnxJoqYVKbn3ngT","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-ux-goal-001/2026-07-24T074300Z-claude-no-skill-3/workspace/packages/hardhat/contracts/MockUSDC.sol","content":"//SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity ^0.8.20;\n\nimport { ERC20 } from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/ERC20.sol\";\n\n/**\n * Mock USDC for local development only.\n *\n * Mirrors the real USDC token (name, symbol, and — importantly — 6 decimals)\n * so the frontend behaves identically against a local chain and against Base.\n * `mint` is intentionally permissionless so any dev account can fund itself.\n *\n * Do NOT deploy this to a live network; on Base the real USDC is used instead.\n */\ncontract MockUSDC is ERC20 {\n    constructor() ERC20(\"USD Coin\", \"USDC\") {}\n\n    function decimals() public pure override returns (uint8) {\n        return 6;\n    }\n\n    function mint(address to, uint256 amount) external {\n        _mint(to, amount);\n    }\n}\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1277,"cache_read_input_tokens":47316,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1277},"output_tokens":2,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"e0f8ee0b-4963-49f1-93b4-a491b6632f5f","uuid":"646c71a1-5545-44ec-825f-b2d63b92984f","timestamp":"2026-07-24T07:47:19.155Z","request_id":"req_011CdLWgVF66dH2zmukUHn2f"}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdLWmzT9AuENBrPz79ygu","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01R1ue8k8KcApgwXqBmbyQA4","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-ux-goal-001/2026-07-24T074300Z-claude-no-skill-3/workspace/packages/hardhat/deploy/00_deploy_staker.ts","content":"import { parseUnits } from \"viem\";\nimport { deployScript, artifacts } from \"../rocketh/deploy.js\";\n\n/**\n * Canonical USDC addresses per chain. Where an entry exists we point the Staker\n * at the real token; otherwise (local/dev chains) we deploy a MockUSDC.\n */\nconst USDC_BY_CHAIN: Record<number, `0x${string}`> = {\n  8453: \"0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913\", // Base mainnet\n  84532: \"0x036CbD53842c5426634e7929541eC2318f3dCF7e\", // Base Sepolia\n};\n\n/**\n * Deploys the Staker contract, wiring it to USDC.\n *\n * - On chains with a known USDC (e.g. Base), the real token is used.\n * - On local/dev chains, a MockUSDC (6 decimals, like real USDC) is deployed\n *   and the deployer is minted a starting balance for testing.\n */\nexport default deployScript(\n  async env => {\n    const { deployer } = env.namedAccounts;\n    const chainId = env.network.chain.id;\n\n    let usdcAddress = USDC_BY_CHAIN[chainId];\n\n    if (!usdcAddress) {\n      const mockUsdc = await env.deploy(\"MockUSDC\", {\n        account: deployer,\n        artifact: artifacts.MockUSDC,\n        args: [],\n      });\n      usdcAddress = mockUsdc.address;\n\n      // Fund the deployer so the /stake page is usable immediately on local.\n      await env.execute(mockUsdc, {\n        account: deployer,\n        functionName: \"mint\",\n        args: [deployer, parseUnits(\"1000000\", 6)],\n      });\n\n      console.log(\"🪙 Deployed MockUSDC at\", usdcAddress);\n    } else {\n      console.log(\"🪙 Using existing USDC at\", usdcAddress);\n    }\n\n    await env.deploy(\"Staker\", {\n      account: deployer,\n      artifact: artifacts.Staker,\n      args: [usdcAddress],\n    });\n  },\n  {\n    tags: [\"Staker\"],\n  },\n);\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":599,"cache_read_input_tokens":57464,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":599},"output_tokens":59,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"e0f8ee0b-4963-49f1-93b4-a491b6632f5f","uuid":"fcbaf697-69c9-4914-a610-0065ff1049d6","timestamp":"2026-07-24T07:48:28.623Z","request_id":"req_011CdLWmxR7EaPVfsdN7BuBB"}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdLWo2i6oXw7BAYYa6HL1","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01NAL8FRtpN1TAmBJgZ2J7fB","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-ux-goal-001/2026-07-24T074300Z-claude-no-skill-3/workspace/packages/hardhat/test/Staker.ts","content":"import { expect } from \"chai\";\nimport { network } from \"hardhat\";\nimport { parseUnits } from \"viem\";\nimport { loadAndExecuteDeploymentsFromFiles } from \"../rocketh/environment.js\";\n\nconst { provider, networkHelpers, ethers } = await network.create();\n\nconst USDC = (n: string) => parseUnits(n, 6);\n\n// Deploy MockUSDC + Staker via the real deploy scripts, then return ethers\n// contract handles wired to distinct signers.\nasync function deployFixture() {\n  const env = await loadAndExecuteDeploymentsFromFiles({ provider });\n\n  const staker = env.get<any>(\"Staker\");\n  const usdc = env.get<any>(\"MockUSDC\");\n\n  const [deployer, alice, bob] = await ethers.getSigners();\n  const stakerC = await ethers.getContractAt(staker.abi, staker.address);\n  const usdcC = await ethers.getContractAt(usdc.abi, usdc.address);\n\n  return { stakerC, usdcC, deployer, alice, bob };\n}\n\ndescribe(\"Staker\", function () {\n  it(\"points at the deployed USDC token\", async function () {\n    const { stakerC, usdcC } = await networkHelpers.loadFixture(deployFixture);\n    expect(await stakerC.usdc()).to.equal(await usdcC.getAddress());\n  });\n\n  it(\"stakes USDC and tracks the position\", async function () {\n    const { stakerC, usdcC, alice } = await networkHelpers.loadFixture(deployFixture);\n    const stakerAddr = await stakerC.getAddress();\n\n    await usdcC.mint(alice.address, USDC(\"100\"));\n    await usdcC.connect(alice).approve(stakerAddr, USDC(\"100\"));\n\n    await expect(stakerC.connect(alice).stake(USDC(\"40\")))\n      .to.emit(stakerC, \"Staked\")\n      .withArgs(alice.address, USDC(\"40\"));\n\n    expect(await stakerC.stakedBalance(alice.address)).to.equal(USDC(\"40\"));\n    expect(await stakerC.totalStaked()).to.equal(USDC(\"40\"));\n    expect(await usdcC.balanceOf(stakerAddr)).to.equal(USDC(\"40\"));\n    expect(await usdcC.balanceOf(alice.address)).to.equal(USDC(\"60\"));\n  });\n\n  it(\"accumulates multiple stakes\", async function () {\n    const { stakerC, usdcC, alice } = await networkHelpers.loadFixture(deployFixture);\n    const stakerAddr = await stakerC.getAddress();\n\n    await usdcC.mint(alice.address, USDC(\"100\"));\n    await usdcC.connect(alice).approve(stakerAddr, USDC(\"100\"));\n\n    await stakerC.connect(alice).stake(USDC(\"30\"));\n    await stakerC.connect(alice).stake(USDC(\"20\"));\n\n    expect(await stakerC.stakedBalance(alice.address)).to.equal(USDC(\"50\"));\n  });\n\n  it(\"withdraws the full position\", async function () {\n    const { stakerC, usdcC, alice } = await networkHelpers.loadFixture(deployFixture);\n    const stakerAddr = await stakerC.getAddress();\n\n    await usdcC.mint(alice.address, USDC(\"100\"));\n    await usdcC.connect(alice).approve(stakerAddr, USDC(\"100\"));\n    await stakerC.connect(alice).stake(USDC(\"100\"));\n\n    await expect(stakerC.connect(alice).withdraw())\n      .to.emit(stakerC, \"Withdrawn\")\n      .withArgs(alice.address, USDC(\"100\"));\n\n    expect(await stakerC.stakedBalance(alice.address)).to.equal(0n);\n    expect(await stakerC.totalStaked()).to.equal(0n);\n    expect(await usdcC.balanceOf(alice.address)).to.equal(USDC(\"100\"));\n    expect(await usdcC.balanceOf(stakerAddr)).to.equal(0n);\n  });\n\n  it(\"keeps user positions isolated\", async function () {\n    const { stakerC, usdcC, alice, bob } = await networkHelpers.loadFixture(deployFixture);\n    const stakerAddr = await stakerC.getAddress();\n\n    await usdcC.mint(alice.address, USDC(\"100\"));\n    await usdcC.mint(bob.address, USDC(\"100\"));\n    await usdcC.connect(alice).approve(stakerAddr, USDC(\"100\"));\n    await usdcC.connect(bob).approve(stakerAddr, USDC(\"100\"));\n\n    await stakerC.connect(alice).stake(USDC(\"70\"));\n    await stakerC.connect(bob).stake(USDC(\"30\"));\n\n    await stakerC.connect(alice).withdraw();\n\n    expect(await stakerC.stakedBalance(alice.address)).to.equal(0n);\n    expect(await stakerC.stakedBalance(bob.address)).to.equal(USDC(\"30\"));\n    expect(await stakerC.totalStaked()).to.equal(USDC(\"30\"));\n  });\n\n  it(\"reverts when staking zero\", async function () {\n    const { stakerC, alice } = await networkHelpers.loadFixture(deployFixture);\n    await expect(stakerC.connect(alice).stake(0n)).to.be.revertedWithCustomError(stakerC, \"ZeroAmount\");\n  });\n\n  it(\"reverts when withdrawing with nothing staked\", async function () {\n    const { stakerC, alice } = await networkHelpers.loadFixture(deployFixture);\n    await expect(stakerC.connect(alice).withdraw()).to.be.revertedWithCustomError(stakerC, \"NothingStaked\");\n  });\n\n  it(\"reverts staking without allowance\", async function () {\n    const { stakerC, usdcC, alice } = await networkHelpers.loadFixture(deployFixture);\n    await usdcC.mint(alice.address, });\n});\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1021,"cache_read_input_tokens":58063,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1021},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"e0f8ee0b-4963-49f1-93b4-a491b6632f5f","uuid":"323c6ad1-c66a-438b-98fc-419ec21300e4","timestamp":"2026-07-24T07:48:50.258Z","request_id":"req_011CdLWo1Xf8dK5z3upJD561"}

contract handles wired to distinct signers.\nasync function deployFixture() {\n  const env = await loadAndExecuteDeploymentsFromFiles({ provider });\n\n  const staker = env.get<any>(\"Staker\");\n  const usdc = env.get<any>(\"MockUSDC\");\n\n  const [deployer, alice, bob] = await ethers.getSigners();\n  const stakerC = await ethers.getContractAt(staker.abi, staker.address);\n  const usdcC = await ethers.getContractAt(usdc.abi, usdc.address);\n\n  return { stakerC, usdcC, deployer, alice, bob };\n}\n\ndescribe(\"Staker\", function () {\n  it(\"points at the deployed USDC token\", async function () {\n    const { stakerC, usdcC } = await networkHelpers.loadFixture(deployFixture);\n    expect(await stakerC.usdc()).to.equal(await usdcC.getAddress());\n  });\n\n  it(\"stakes USDC and tracks the position\", async function () {\n    const { stakerC, usdcC, alice } = await networkHelpers.loadFixture(deployFixture);\n    const stakerAddr = await stakerC.getAddress();\n\n    await usdcC.mint(alice.address, USDC(\"100\"));\n    await usdcC.connect(alice).approve(stakerAddr, USDC(\"100\"));\n\n    await expect(stakerC.connect(alice).stake(USDC(\"40\")))\n      .to.emit(stakerC, \"Staked\")\n      .withArgs(alice.address, USDC(\"40\"));\n\n    expect(await stakerC.stakedBalance(alice.address)).to.equal(USDC(\"40\"));\n    expect(await stakerC.totalStaked()).to.equal(USDC(\"40\"));\n    expect(await usdcC.balanceOf(stakerAddr)).to.equal(USDC(\"40\"));\n    expect(await usdcC.balanceOf(alice.address)).to.equal(USDC(\"60\"));\n  });\n\n  it(\"accumulates multiple stakes\", async function () {\n    const { stakerC, usdcC, alice } = await networkHelpers.loadFixture(deployFixture);\n    const stakerAddr = await stakerC.getAddress();\n\n    await usdcC.mint(alice.address, USDC(\"100\"));\n    await usdcC.connect(alice).approve(stakerAddr, USDC(\"100\"));\n\n    await stakerC.connect(alice).stake(USDC(\"30\"));\n    await stakerC.connect(alice).stake(USDC(\"20\"));\n\n    expect(await stakerC.stakedBalance(alice.address)).to.equal(USDC(\"50\"));\n  });\n\n  it(\"withdraws the full position\", async function () {\n    const { stakerC, usdcC, alice } = await networkHelpers.loadFixture(deployFixture);\n    const stakerAddr = await stakerC.getAddress();\n\n    await usdcC.mint(alice.address, USDC(\"100\"));\n    await usdcC.connect(alice).approve(stakerAddr, USDC(\"100\"));\n    await stakerC.connect(alice).stake(USDC(\"100\"));\n\n    await expect(stakerC.connect(alice).withdraw())\n      .to.emit(stakerC, \"Withdrawn\")\n      .withArgs(alice.address, USDC(\"100\"));\n\n    expect(await stakerC.stakedBalance(alice.address)).to.equal(0n);\n    expect(await stakerC.totalStaked()).to.equal(0n);\n    expect(await usdcC.balanceOf(alice.address)).to.equal(USDC(\"100\"));\n    expect(await usdcC.balanceOf(stakerAddr)).to.equal(0n);\n  });\n\n  it(\"keeps user positions isolated\", async function () {\n    const { stakerC, usdcC, alice, bob } = await networkHelpers.loadFixture(deployFixture);\n    const stakerAddr = await stakerC.getAddress();\n\n    await usdcC.mint(alice.address, USDC(\"100\"));\n    await usdcC.mint(bob.address, USDC(\"100\"));\n    await usdcC.connect(alice).approve(stakerAddr, USDC(\"100\"));\n    await usdcC.connect(bob).approve(stakerAddr, USDC(\"100\"));\n\n    await stakerC.connect(alice).stake(USDC(\"70\"));\n    await stakerC.connect(bob).stake(USDC(\"30\"));\n\n    await stakerC.connect(alice).withdraw();\n\n    expect(await stakerC.stakedBalance(alice.address)).to.equal(0n);\n    expect(await stakerC.stakedBalance(bob.address)).to.equal(USDC(\"30\"));\n    expect(await stakerC.totalStaked()).to.equal(USDC(\"30\"));\n  });\n\n  it(\"reverts when staking zero\", async function () {\n    const { stakerC, alice } = await networkHelpers.loadFixture(deployFixture);\n    await expect(stakerC.connect(alice).stake(0n)).to.be.revertedWithCustomError(stakerC, \"ZeroAmount\");\n  });\n\n  it(\"reverts when withdrawing with nothing staked\", async function () {\n    const { stakerC, alice } = await networkHelpers.loadFixture(deployFixture);\n    await expect(stakerC.connect(alice).withdraw()).to.be.revertedWithCustomError(stakerC, \"NothingStaked\");\n  });\n\n  it(\"reverts staking without allowance\", async function () {\n    const { stakerC, usdcC, alice } = await networkHelpers.loadFixture(deployFixture);\n    await usdcC.mint(alice.address, USDC(\"100\"));\n    // no approve\n    await expect(stakerC.connect(alice).stake(USDC(\"10\"))).to.be.reverted;\n  });\n});\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}

usdcC.getAddress()).toLowerCase());","originalFile":"import { expect } from \"chai\";\nimport { network } from \"hardhat\";\nimport { parseUnits } from \"viem\";\nimport { loadAndExecuteDeploymentsFromFiles } from \"../rocketh/environment.js\";\n\nconst { provider, networkHelpers, ethers } = await network.create();\n\nconst USDC = (n: string) => parseUnits(n, 6);\n\n// Deploy MockUSDC + Staker via the real deploy scripts, then return ethers\n// contract handles wired to distinct signers.\nasync function deployFixture() {\n  const env = await loadAndExecuteDeploymentsFromFiles({ provider });\n\n  const staker = env.get<any>(\"Staker\");\n  const usdc = env.get<any>(\"MockUSDC\");\n\n  const [deployer, alice, bob] = await ethers.getSigners();\n  const stakerC = await ethers.getContractAt(staker.abi, staker.address);\n  const usdcC = await ethers.getContractAt(usdc.abi, usdc.address);\n\n  return { stakerC, usdcC, deployer, alice, bob };\n}\n\ndescribe(\"Staker\", function () {\n  it(\"points at the deployed USDC token\", async function () {\n    const { stakerC, usdcC } = await networkHelpers.loadFixture(deployFixture);\n    expect(await stakerC.usdc()).to.equal(await usdcC.getAddress());\n  });\n\n  it(\"stakes USDC and tracks the position\", async function () {\n    const { stakerC, usdcC, alice } = await networkHelpers.loadFixture(deployFixture);\n    const stakerAddr = await stakerC.getAddress();\n\n    await usdcC.mint(alice.address, USDC(\"100\"));\n    await usdcC.connect(alice).approve(stakerAddr, USDC(\"100\"));\n\n    await expect(stakerC.connect(alice).stake(USDC(\"40\")))\n      .to.emit(stakerC, \"Staked\")\n      .withArgs(alice.address, USDC(\"40\"));\n\n    expect(await stakerC.stakedBalance(alice.address)).to.equal(USDC(\"40\"));\n    expect(await stakerC.totalStaked()).to.equal(USDC(\"40\"));\n    expect(await usdcC.balanceOf(stakerAddr)).to.equal(USDC(\"40\"));\n    expect(await usdcC.balanceOf(alice.address)).to.equal(USDC(\"60\"));\n  });\n\n  it(\"accumulates multiple stakes\", async function () {\n    const { stakerC, usdcC, alice } = await networkHelpers.loadFixture(deployFixture);\n    const stakerAddr = await stakerC.getAddress();\n\n    await usdcC.mint(alice.address, USDC(\"100\"));\n    await usdcC.connect(alice).approve(stakerAddr, USDC(\"100\"));\n\n    await stakerC.connect(alice).stake(USDC(\"30\"));\n    await stakerC.connect(alice).stake(USDC(\"20\"));\n\n    expect(await stakerC.stakedBalance(alice.address)).to.equal(USDC(\"50\"));\n  });\n\n  it(\"withdraws the full position\", async function () {\n    const { stakerC, usdcC, alice } = await networkHelpers.loadFixture(deployFixture);\n    const stakerAddr = await stakerC.getAddress();\n\n    await usdcC.mint(alice.address, USDC(\"100\"));\n    await usdcC.connect(alice).approve(stakerAddr, USDC(\"100\"));\n    await stakerC.connect(alice).stake(USDC(\"100\"));\n\n    await expect(stakerC.connect(alice).withdraw())\n      .to.emit(stakerC, \"Withdrawn\")\n      .withArgs(alice.address, USDC(\"100\"));\n\n    expect(await stakerC.stakedBalance(alice.address)).to.equal(0n);\n    expect(await stakerC.totalStaked()).to.equal(0n);\n    expect(await usdcC.balanceOf(alice.address)).to.equal(USDC(\"100\"));\n    expect(await usdcC.balanceOf(stakerAddr)).to.equal(0n);\n  });\n\n  it(\"keeps user positions isolated\", async function () {\n    const { stakerC, usdcC, alice, bob } = await networkHelpers.loadFixture(deployFixture);\n    const stakerAddr = await stakerC.getAddress();\n\n    await usdcC.mint(alice.address, USDC(\"100\"));\n    await usdcC.mint(bob.address, USDC(\"100\"));\n    await usdcC.connect(alice).approve(stakerAddr, USDC(\"100\"));\n    await usdcC.connect(bob).approve(stakerAddr, USDC(\"100\"));\n\n    await stakerC.connect(alice).stake(USDC(\"70\"));\n    await stakerC.connect(bob).stake(USDC(\"30\"));\n\n    await stakerC.connect(alice).withdraw();\n\n    expect(await stakerC.stakedBalance(alice.address)).to.equal(0n);\n    expect(await stakerC.stakedBalance(bob.address)).to.equal(USDC(\"30\"));\n    expect(await stakerC.totalStaked()).to.equal(USDC(\"30\"));\n  });\n\n  it(\"reverts when staking zero\", async function () {\n    const { stakerC, alice } = await networkHelpers.loadFixture(deployFixture);\n    await expect(stakerC.connect(alice).stake(0n)).to.be.revertedWithCustomError(stakerC, \"ZeroAmount\");\n  });\n\n  it(\"reverts when withdrawing with nothing staked\", async function () {\n    const { stakerC, alice } = await networkHelpers.loadFixture(deployFixture);\n    await expect(stakerC.connect(alice).withdraw()).to.be.revertedWithCustomError(stakerC, \"NothingStaked\");\n  });\n\n  it(\"reverts staking without allowance\", async function () {\n    const { stakerC, usdcC, alice } = await networkHelpers.loadFixture(deployFixture);\n    await usdcC.mint(alice.address, USDC(\"100\"));\n    // no approve\n    await expect(stakerC.connect(alice).stake(USDC(\"10\"))).to.be.reverted;\n  });\n});\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":25,"oldLines":7,"newStart":25,"newLines":7,"lines":[" describe(\"Staker\", function () {","   it(\"points at the deployed USDC token\", async function () .withArgs(alice.address, USDC(\"40\"));\n\n    expect(await stakerC.stakedBalance(alice.address)).to.equal(USDC(\"40\"));\n    expect(await stakerC.totalStaked()).to.equal(USDC(\"40\"));\n {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01YSxfiXtjhh2Pm6uM9nh65d","type":"tool_result","content":"index.ts\nuseContractLogs.ts\nuseCopyToClipboard.ts\nuseDeployedContractInfo.ts\nuseFetchBlocks.ts\nuseNetworkColor.ts\nuseOutsideClick.ts\nuseScaffoldContract.ts\nuseScaffoldEventHistory.ts\nuseScaffoldReadContract.ts\nuseScaffoldWatchContractEvent.ts\nuseScaffoldWriteContract.ts\nuseSelectedNetwork.ts\nuseTargetNetwork.ts\nuseTransactor.tsx\n=== useTransactor ===\n/Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-ux-goal-001/2026-07-24T074300Z-claude-no-skill-3/workspace/packages/nextjs/hooks/scaffold-eth/useTransactor.tsx\nimport { Hash, SendTransactionParameters, TransactionReceipt, WalletClient } from \"viem\";\nimport { Config, useWalletClient } from \"wagmi\";\nimport { getPublicClient } from \"wagmi/actions\";\nimport { SendTransactionMutate } from \"wagmi/query\";\nimport scaffoldConfig from \"~~/scaffold.config\";\nimport { wagmiConfig } from \"~~/services/web3/wagmiConfig\";\nimport { AllowedChainIds, getBlockExplorerTxLink, notification } from \"~~/utils/scaffold-eth\";\nimport { TransactorFuncOptions, getParsedErrorWithAllAbis } from \"~~/utils/scaffold-eth/contract\";\n\ntype TransactionFunc = (\n  tx: (() => Promise<Hash>) | Parameters<SendTransactionMutate<Config, undefined>>[0],\n  options?: TransactorFuncOptions,\n) => Promise<Hash | undefined>;\n\n/**\n * Custom notification content for TXs.\n */\nconst TxnNotification = ({ message, blockExplorerLink }: { message: string; blockExplorerLink?: string }) => {\n  return (\n    <div className={`flex flex-col ml-1 cursor-default`}>\n      <p className=\"my-0\">{message}</p>\n      {blockExplorerLink && blockExplorerLink.length > 0 ? (\n        <a href={blockExplorerLink} target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noreferrer\" className=\"block link\">\n          check out transaction\n        </a>\n      ) : null}\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n\n/**\n * Runs Transaction passed in to returned function showing UI feedback.\n * @param _walletClient - Optional wallet client to use. If not provided, will use the one from useWalletClient.\n * @returns function that takes in transaction function as callback, shows UI feedback for transaction and returns a promise of the transaction hash\n */\nexport const useTransactor = (_walletClient?: WalletClient): TransactionFunc => {\n  let walletClient = _walletClient;\n  const { data } = useWalletClient();\n  if (walletClient === undefined && data) {\n    walletClient = data;\n  }\n\n  const result: TransactionFunc = async (tx, options) => {\n    if (!walletClient) {\n      notification.error(\"Cannot access account\");\n      console.error(\"⚡️ ~ file: useTransactor.tsx ~ error\");\n      return;\n    }\n\n    let notificationId = null;\n    let transactionHash: Hash | undefined = undefined;\n    let transactionReceipt: TransactionReceipt | undefined;\n    let blockExplorerTxURL = \"\";\n    let chainId: number = scaffoldConfig.targetNetworks[0].id;\n    try {\n      chainId = await walletClient.getChainId();\n      // Get full transaction from public client\n      const publicClient = getPublicClient(wagmiConfig);\n\n      notificationId = notification.loading(<TxnNotification message=\"Awaiting for user confirmation\" />);\n      if (typeof tx === \"function\") {\n        // Tx is already prepared by the caller\n        const result = await tx();\n        transactionHash = result;\n      } else if (tx != null) {\n        transactionHash = await walletClient.sendTransaction(tx as SendTransactionParameters);\n      } else {\n        throw new Error(\"Incorrect transaction passed to transactor\");\n      }\n      notification.remove(notificationId);","is_error":false}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"e0f8ee0b-4963-49f1-93b4-a491b6632f5f","uuid":"080feff0-a034-499f-9b4c-c9850551b4f2","timestamp":"2026-07-24T07:51:52.106Z","tool_use_result":{"stdout":"index.ts\nuseContractLogs.ts\nuseCopyToClipboard.ts\nuseDeployedContractInfo.ts\nuseFetchBlocks.ts\nuseNetworkColor.ts\nuseOutsideClick.ts\nuseScaffoldContract.ts\nuseScaffoldEventHistory.ts\nuseScaffoldReadContract.ts\nuseScaffoldWatchContractEvent.ts\nuseScaffoldWriteContract.ts\nuseSelectedNetwork.ts\nuseTargetNetwork.ts\nuseTransactor.tsx\n=== {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01V5RzMvZNdpCxD8pnA8vMbt","type":"tool_result","content":"=== useScaffoldWriteContract (head) ===\nimport { useEffect, useState } from \"react\";\nimport { MutateOptions } from \"@tanstack/react-query\";\nimport { Abi, ExtractAbiFunctionNames } from \"abitype\";\nimport { Config, UseWriteContractParameters, useAccount, useConfig, useWriteContract } from \"wagmi\";\nimport { WriteContractErrorType, WriteContractReturnType } from \"wagmi/actions\";\nimport { WriteContractVariables } from \"wagmi/query\";\nimport { useSelectedNetwork } from \"~~/hooks/scaffold-eth\";\nimport { useDeployedContractInfo, useTransactor } from \"~~/hooks/scaffold-eth\";\nimport { AllowedChainIds, notification } from \"~~/utils/scaffold-eth\";\nimport {\n  ContractAbi,\n  ContractName,\n  ScaffoldWriteContractOptions,\n  ScaffoldWriteContractVariables,\n  UseScaffoldWriteConfig,\n  simulateContractWriteAndNotifyError,\n} from \"~~/utils/scaffold-eth/contract\";\n\ntype ScaffoldWriteContractReturnType<TContractName extends ContractName> = Omit<\n  ReturnType<typeof useWriteContract>,\n  \"writeContract\" | \"writeContractAsync\"\n> & {\n  isMining: boolean;\n  writeContractAsync: <\n    TFunctionName extends ExtractAbiFunctionNames<ContractAbi<TContractName>, \"nonpayable\" | \"payable\">,\n  >(\n    variables: ScaffoldWriteContractVariables<TContractName, TFunctionName>,\n    options?: ScaffoldWriteContractOptions,\n  ) => Promise<WriteContractReturnType | undefined>;\n  writeContract: <TFunctionName extends ExtractAbiFunctionNames<ContractAbi<TContractName>, \"nonpayable\" | \"payable\">>(\n    variables: ScaffoldWriteContractVariables<TContractName, TFunctionName>,\n    options?: Omit<ScaffoldWriteContractOptions, \"onBlockConfirmation\" | \"blockConfirmations\">,\n  ) => void;\n};\n\nexport function useScaffoldWriteContract<TContractName extends ContractName>(\n  config: UseScaffoldWriteConfig<TContractName>,\n): ScaffoldWriteContractReturnType<TContractName>;\n/**\n * @deprecated Use object parameter version instead: useScaffoldWriteContract({ contractName: \"YourContract\" })\n */\nexport function useScaffoldWriteContract<TContractName extends ContractName>(\n  contractName: TContractName,\n  writeContractParams?: UseWriteContractParameters,\n): ScaffoldWriteContractReturnType<TContractName>;\n\n/**\n * Wrapper around wagmi's useWriteContract hook which automatically loads (by name) the contract ABI and address from\n * the contracts present in deployedContracts.ts & externalContracts.ts corresponding to targetNetworks configured in scaffold.config.ts\n * @param contractName - name of the contract to be written to\n * @param config.chainId - optional chainId that is configured with the scaffold project to make use for multi-chain interactions.\n * @param writeContractParams - wagmi's useWriteContract parameters\n */\nexport function useScaffoldWriteContract<TContractName extends ContractName>(\n  configOrName: UseScaffoldWriteConfig<TContractName> | TContractName,\n  writeContractParams?: UseWriteContractParameters,\n): ScaffoldWriteContractReturnType<TContractName> {\n  const finalConfig =\n    typeof configOrName === \"string\"\n      ? { contractName: configOrName, writeContractParams, chainId: undefined }\n      : (configOrName as UseScaffoldWriteConfig<TContractName>);\n  const { contractName, chainId, writeContractParams: finalWriteContractParams } = finalConfig;\n\n  const wagmiConfig = useConfig();\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (typeof configOrName === \"string\") {\n      console.warn(\n        \"Using `useScaffoldWriteContract` with a string parameter is deprecated. Please use the object parameter version instead.\",\n      );\n    }\n  }, [configOrName]);\n\n  const { chain: accountChain } = useAccount();\n  const writeTx = useTransactor();\n  const [isMining, setIsMining] = useState(false);\n\n  const wagmiContractWrite = useWriteContract(finalWriteContractParams);\n\n  const selectedNetwork = useSelectedNetwork(chainId);","is_error":false}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"e0f8ee0b-4963-49f1-93b4-a491b6632f5f","uuid":"dcf474e8-5373-4b70-87b0-658de1f91602","timestamp":"2026-07-24T07:52:03.637Z","tool_use_result":{"stdout":"=== {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01UgfJfRKkRZyqeExScURVCN","type":"tool_result","content":"=== useScaffoldContract ===\nimport { Account, Address, Chain, Client, Transport, getContract } from \"viem\";\nimport { usePublicClient } from \"wagmi\";\nimport { GetWalletClientReturnType } from \"wagmi/actions\";\nimport { useSelectedNetwork } from \"~~/hooks/scaffold-eth\";\nimport { useDeployedContractInfo } from \"~~/hooks/scaffold-eth\";\nimport { AllowedChainIds } from \"~~/utils/scaffold-eth\";\nimport { Contract, ContractName } from \"~~/utils/scaffold-eth/contract\";\n\n/**\n * Gets a viem instance of the contract present in deployedContracts.ts or externalContracts.ts corresponding to\n * targetNetworks configured in scaffold.config.ts. Optional walletClient can be passed for doing write transactions.\n * @param config - The config settings for the hook\n * @param config.contractName - deployed contract name\n * @param config.walletClient - optional walletClient from wagmi useWalletClient hook can be passed for doing write transactions\n * @param config.chainId - optional chainId that is configured with the scaffold project to make use for multi-chain interactions.\n */\nexport const useScaffoldContract = <\n  TContractName extends ContractName,\n  TWalletClient extends Exclude<GetWalletClientReturnType, null> | undefined,\n>({\n  contractName,\n  walletClient,\n  chainId,\n}: {\n  contractName: TContractName;\n  walletClient?: TWalletClient | null;\n  chainId?: AllowedChainIds;\n}) => {\n  const selectedNetwork = useSelectedNetwork(chainId);\n  const { data: deployedContractData, isLoading: deployedContractLoading } = useDeployedContractInfo({\n    contractName,\n    chainId: selectedNetwork?.id as AllowedChainIds,\n  });\n\n  const publicClient = usePublicClient({ chainId: selectedNetwork?.id });\n\n  let contract = undefined;\n  if (deployedContractData && publicClient) {\n    contract = getContract<\n      Transport,\n      Address,\n      Contract<TContractName>[\"abi\"],\n      TWalletClient extends Exclude<GetWalletClientReturnType, null>\n        ? {\n            public: Client<Transport, Chain>;\n            wallet: TWalletClient;\n          }\n        : { public: Client<Transport, Chain> },\n      Chain,\n      Account\n    >({\n      address: deployedContractData.address,\n      abi: deployedContractData.abi as Contract<TContractName>[\"abi\"],\n      client: {\n        public: publicClient,\n        wallet: walletClient ? walletClient : undefined,\n      } as any,\n    });\n  }\n\n=== useScaffoldReadContract head ===\nimport { useEffect } from \"react\";\nimport { QueryObserverResult, RefetchOptions, useQueryClient } from \"@tanstack/react-query\";\nimport type { ExtractAbiFunctionNames } from \"abitype\";\nimport { ReadContractErrorType } from \"viem\";\nimport { useBlockNumber, useReadContract } from \"wagmi\";\nimport { useSelectedNetwork } from \"~~/hooks/scaffold-eth\";\nimport { useDeployedContractInfo } from \"~~/hooks/scaffold-eth\";\nimport { AllowedChainIds } from \"~~/utils/scaffold-eth\";\nimport {\n  AbiFunctionReturnType,\n  ContractAbi,\n  ContractName,\n  UseScaffoldReadConfig,\n} from \"~~/utils/scaffold-eth/contract\";\n\n/**\n * Wrapper around wagmi's useContractRead hook which automatically loads (by name) the contract ABI and address from\n * the contracts present in deployedContracts.ts & externalContracts.ts corresponding to targetNetworks configured in scaffold.config.ts\n * @param config - The config settings, including extra wagmi configuration\n * @param config.contractName - deployed contract name\n * @param config.functionName - name of the function to be called\n * @param config.args - args to be passed to the function call\n * @param config.chainId - optional chainId that is configured with the scaffold project to make use for multi-chain interactions.\n */\nexport const useScaffoldReadContract = <\n  TContractName extends ContractName,\n  TFunctionName extends ExtractAbiFunctionNames<ContractAbi<TContractName>, \"pure\" | \"view\">,\n>({\n  contractName,\n  functionName,\n  args,\n  chainId,\n  ...readConfig\n}: UseScaffoldReadConfig<TContractName, TFunctionName>) => {\n  const selectedNetwork = useSelectedNetwork(chainId);\n  const { data: deployedContract } = useDeployedContractInfo({\n    contractName,\n    chainId: selectedNetwork.id as AllowedChainIds,\n  });","is_error":false}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"e0f8ee0b-4963-49f1-93b4-a491b6632f5f","uuid":"c3c8cd14-5ebd-4339-a9c5-2c6865421944","timestamp":"2026-07-24T07:53:20.441Z","tool_use_result":{"stdout":"=== useScaffoldContract ===\nimport { Account, Address, Chain, Client, Transport, getContract } from \"viem\";\nimport { usePublicClient } from \"wagmi\";\nimport { GetWalletClientReturnType } from \"wagmi/actions\";\nimport { useSelectedNetwork } from \"~~/hooks/scaffold-eth\";\nimport { useDeployedContractInfo } from \"~~/hooks/scaffold-eth\";\nimport { AllowedChainIds } from \"~~/utils/scaffold-eth\";\nimport { Contract, ContractName } from \"~~/utils/scaffold-eth/contract\";\n\n/**\n * Gets a viem instance of the contract present in deployedContracts.ts or externalContracts.ts corresponding to\n * targetNetworks configured in scaffold.config.ts. Optional walletClient can be passed for doing write transactions.\n * @param config - The config settings for the hook\n * @param config.contractName - deployed contract name\n * @param config.walletClient - optional walletClient from wagmi useWalletClient hook can be passed for doing write transactions\n * @param config.chainId - optional chainId that is configured with the scaffold project to make use for multi-chain interactions.\n */\nexport const useScaffoldContract = <\n  TContractName extends ContractName,\n  TWalletClient extends Exclude<GetWalletClientReturnType, null> | undefined,\n>({\n  contractName,\n  walletClient,\n  chainId,\n}: {\n  contractName: TContractName;\n  walletClient?: TWalletClient | null;\n  chainId?: AllowedChainIds;\n}) => {\n  const selectedNetwork = useSelectedNetwork(chainId);\n  const { data: deployedContractData, isLoading: deployedContractLoading } = useDeployedContractInfo({\n    contractName,\n    chainId: selectedNetwork?.id as AllowedChainIds,\n  });\n\n  const publicClient = usePublicClient({ chainId: selectedNetwork?.id });\n\n  let contract = undefined;\n  if (deployedContractData && publicClient) {\n    contract = getContract<\n      Transport,\n      Address,\n      Contract<TContractName>[\"abi\"],\n      TWalletClient extends Exclude<GetWalletClientReturnType, null>\n        ? {\n            public: Client<Transport, Chain>;\n            wallet: TWalletClient;\n          }\n        : { public: Client<Transport, Chain> },\n      Chain,\n      Account\n    >({\n      address: deployedContractData.address,\n      abi: deployedContractData.abi as Contract<TContractName>[\"abi\"],\n      client: {\n        public: publicClient,\n        wallet: walletClient ? walletClient : undefined,\n      } as any,\n    });\n  }\n\n=== useScaffoldReadContract head ===\nimport { useEffect } from \"react\";\nimport { QueryObserverResult, RefetchOptions, useQueryClient } from \"@tanstack/react-query\";\nimport type { ExtractAbiFunctionNames } from \"abitype\";\nimport { ReadContractErrorType } from \"viem\";\nimport { useBlockNumber, useReadContract } from \"wagmi\";\nimport { useSelectedNetwork } from \"~~/hooks/scaffold-eth\";\nimport { useDeployedContractInfo } from \"~~/hooks/scaffold-eth\";\nimport { AllowedChainIds } from \"~~/utils/scaffold-eth\";\nimport {\n  AbiFunctionReturnType,\n  ContractAbi,\n  ContractName,\n  UseScaffoldReadConfig,\n} from \"~~/utils/scaffold-eth/contract\";\n\n/**\n * Wrapper around wagmi's useContractRead hook which automatically loads (by name) the contract ABI and address from\n * the contracts present in deployedContracts.ts & externalContracts.ts corresponding to targetNetworks configured in scaffold.config.ts\n * @param config - The config settings, including extra wagmi configuration\n * @param config.contractName - deployed contract name\n * @param config.functionName - name of the function to be called\n * @param config.args - args to be passed to the function call\n * @param config.chainId - optional chainId that is configured with the scaffold project to make use for multi-chain interactions.\n */\nexport const useScaffoldReadContract = <\n  TContractName extends ContractName,\n  TFunctionName extends ExtractAbiFunctionNames<ContractAbi<TContractName>, \"pure\" | \"view\">,\n>({\n  contractName,\n  functionName,\n  args,\n  chainId,\n  ...readConfig\n}: UseScaffoldReadConfig<TContractName, TFunctionName>) => {\n  const selectedNetwork = useSelectedNetwork(chainId);\n  const { data: deployedContract } = useDeployedContractInfo({\n    contractName,\n    chainId: selectedNetwork.id as AllowedChainIds,\n  });","stderr":"","interrupted":false,"isImage":false,"noOutputExpected":false}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdLXFx6HrXsRVjWWZcxwi","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01JhpwnLeN8ugFzjGouF4kxn","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-ux-goal-001/2026-07-24T074300Z-claude-no-skill-3/workspace/packages/nextjs/app/stake/page.tsx","content":"\"use client\";\n\nimport { useMemo, useState } from \"react\";\nimport type { NextPage } from \"next\";\nimport { erc20Abi, formatUnits, parseUnits } from \"viem\";\nimport { useAccount, useReadContract, useWriteContract } from \"wagmi\";\nimport { Address } from \"@scaffold-ui/components\";\nimport { useWatchBalance } from \"@scaffold-ui/hooks\";\nimport {\n  useDeployedContractInfo,\n  useScaffoldReadContract,\n  useScaffoldWriteContract,\n  useTargetNetwork,\n  useTransactor,\n} from \"~~/hooks/scaffold-eth\";\n\n// Trim a token amount to a readable number of decimals.\nconst formatAmount = (value: bigint | undefined, decimals: number, maxFractionDigits = 2) => {\n  if (value === undefined) return \"0\";\n  return Number(formatUnits(value, decimals)).toLocaleString(undefined, {\n    maximumFractionDigits: maxFractionDigits,\n  });\n};\n\nconst Stake: NextPage = () => {\n  const { address, isConnected } = useAccount();\n  const { targetNetwork } = useTargetNetwork();\n\n  const { data: stakerInfo, isLoading: stakerInfoLoading } = useDeployedContractInfo({ contractName: \"Staker\" });\n  const stakerAddress = stakerInfo?.address;\n\n  // The token the Staker custodies is the single source of truth — no hardcoded\n  // address in the UI, so this works against Base and a local mock alike.\n  const { data: usdcAddress } = useScaffoldReadContract({ contractName: \"Staker\", functionName: \"usdc\" });\n\n  const { data: ethBalance } = useWatchBalance({ address, chain: targetNetwork });\n  const { data: usdcBalance } = useWatchBalance({\n    address,\n    token: usdcAddress,\n    chain: targetNetwork,\n    query: { enabled: Boolean(address && usdcAddress) },\n  });\n  const decimals = usdcBalance?.decimals ?? 6;\n  const symbol = usdcBalance?.symbol ?? \"USDC\";\n\n  const { data: staked, refetch: refetchStaked } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"Staker\",\n    functionName: \"stakedBalance\",\n    args: [address],\n  });\n\n  const { data: allowance, refetch: refetchAllowance } = useReadContract({\n    address: usdcAddress,\n    abi: erc20Abi,\n    functionName: \"allowance\",\n    args: address && stakerAddress ? [address, stakerAddress] : undefined,\n    chainId: targetNetwork.id,\n    query: { enabled: Boolean(address && stakerAddress && usdcAddress) },\n  });\n\n  const { writeContractAsync: writeStaker, isMining } = useScaffoldWriteContract({ contractName: \"Staker\" });\n  const { writeContractAsync: writeErc20 } = useWriteContract();\n  const writeTx = useTransactor();\n  const [approving, setApproving] = useState(false);\n\n  const [amount, setAmount] = useState(\"\");\n  const parsedAmount = useMemo(() => {\n    try {\n      return amount ? parseUnits(amount, decimals) : 0n;\n    } catch {\n      return 0n;\n    }\n  }, [amount, decimals]);\n\n  const walletValue = usdcBalance?.value ?? 0n;\n  const stakedValue = staked ?? 0n;\n  const insufficientBalance = parsedAmount > walletValue;\n  const needsApproval = parsedAmount > (allowance ?? 0n);\n  const busy = isMining || approving;\n  const canStake = isConnected && !!stakerAddress && parsedAmount > 0n && !insufficientBalance && !busy;\n\n  const setMax = () => setAmount(formatUnits(walletValue, decimals));\n\n  const handleApprove = async () => {\n    if (!usdcAddress || !stakerAddress || parsedAmount === 0n) return;\n    try {\n      setApproving(true);\n      await writeTx(() =>\n        writeErc20({\n          address: usdcAddress,\n          abi: erc20Abi,\n          functionName: \"approve\",\n          args: [stakerAddress, parsedAmount],\n        }),\n      );\n      await refetchAllowance();\n    } catch (e) {\n      console.error(\"Approve failed\", e);\n    } finally {\n      setApproving(false);\n    }\n  };\n\n  const handleStake = async () => {\n    try {\n      await writeStaker({ functionName: \"stake\", args: [parsedAmount] });\n      setAmount(\"\");\n      // Staking consumes the allowance, so refresh it alongside the position.\n      await Promise.all([refetchStaked(), refetchAllowance()]);\n    } catch (e) {\n      console.error(\"Stake failed\", e);\n    }\n  };\n\n  const handleWithdraw = async () => {\n    try {\n      await writeStaker({ functionName: \"withdraw\" });\n      await refetchStaked();\n    } catch (e) {\n      console.error(\"Withdraw failed\", e);\n    }\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"flex flex-col items-center grow w-full px-4 py-10\">\n      <div className=\"w-full max-w-xl flex flex-col gap-6\">\n        <div className=\"text-center\">\n          <h1 className=\"text-4xl font-bold mb-1\">Stake USDC</h1>\n          <p className=\"text-base-content/70\">Deposit USDC and withdraw your full position anytime. No lockups.</p>\n        </div>\n\n        {isConnected && (\n          <div className=\"flex items-center justify-center gap-2 text-sm\">\n            <span className=\"text-base-content/70\">Wallet:</span>\n            <Address address={address} chain={targetNetwork} />\n          </div>\n        )}\n\n        <div className=\"stats stats-vertical sm:stats-horizontal shadow bg-base-100 w-full\">\n          <div className=\"stat\">\n            <div className=\"stat-title\">Wallet {symbol}</div>\n            <div className=\"stat-value text-2xl\">{formatAmount(walletValue, decimals)}</div>\n          </div>\n          <div className=\"stat\">\n            <div className=\"stat-title\">ETH (for gas)</div>\n            <div className=\"stat-value text-2xl\">{formatAmount(ethBalance?.value, ethBalance?.decimals ?? 18, 4)}</div>\n          </div>\n          <div className=\"stat\">\n            <div className=\"stat-title\">Staked {symbol}</div>\n            <div className=\"stat-value text-2xl text-primary\">{formatAmount(stakedValue, decimals)}</div>\n          </div>\n        </div>\n\n        {!isConnected ? (\n          <div className=\"card bg-base-100 shadow-xl\">\n            <div className=\"card-body items-center text-center\">\n              <p>Connect your wallet to stake.</p>\n            </div>\n          </div>\n        ) : !stakerAddress && !stakerInfoLoading ? (\n          <div className=\"card bg-base-100 shadow-xl\">\n            <div className=\"card-body items-center text-center\">\n              <p>Staker contract is not deployed on {targetNetwork.name}.</p>\n            </div>\n          </div>\n        ) : (\n          <>\n            <div className=\"card bg-base-100 shadow-xl\">\n              <div className=\"card-body gap-4\">\n                <h2 className=\"card-title\">Stake</h2>\n                <div className=\"form-control\">\n                  <label className=\"input input-bordered flex items-center gap-2\">\n                    <input\n                      type=\"number\"\n                      min=\"0\"\n                      step=\"any\"\n                      placeholder=\"0.00\"\n                      className=\"grow\"\n                      value={amount}\n                      onChange={e => setAmount(e.target.value)}\n                    />\n                    <span className=\"text-base-content/60\">{symbol}</span>\n                    <button type=\"button\" className=\"btn btn-xs btn-ghost\" onClick={setMax}>\n                      Max\n                    </button>\n                  </label>\n                  {insufficientBalance && <span className=\"text-error text-sm mt-1\">Amount exceeds wallet balance.</span>}\n                </div>\n\n                {needsApproval && parsedAmount > 0n && !insufficientBalance ? (\n                  <button className=\"btn btn-primary\" onClick={handleApprove} disabled={busy}>\n                    {approving && <span className=\"loading loading-spinner loading-sm\" />}\n                    Approve {symbol}\n                  </button>\n                ) : (\n                  <button className=\"btn btn-primary\" onClick={handleStake} disabled={!canStake}>\n                    {isMining && <span className=\"loading loading-spinner loading-sm\" />}\n                    Stake\n                  </button>\n                )}\n              </div>\n            </div>\n\n            <div className=\"card bg-base-100 shadow-xl\">\n              <div className=\"card-body gap-4\">\n                <h2 className=\"card-title\">Your position</h2>\n                <p className=\"text-lg\">\n                  <span className=\"font-bold\">{formatAmount(stakedValue, decimals)}</span> {symbol} staked\n                </p>\n                <button\n                  className=\"btn btn-secondary\"\n                  onClick={handleWithdraw}\n                  disabled={stakedValue === 0n || busy}\n                >\n                  {isMining && <span className=\"loading loading-spinner loading-sm\" />}\n                  Withdraw all\n                </button>\n              </div>\n            </div>\n          </>\n        )}\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n\nexport default Stake;\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":6025,"cache_read_input_tokens":84995,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":6025},"output_tokens":2,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"e0f8ee0b-4963-49f1-93b4-a491b6632f5f","uuid":"a526dbe9-6444-4481-98da-c365b82c083c","timestamp":"2026-07-24T07:55:09.472Z","request_id":"req_011CdLXFvHQ2Rc9MurwU8mt2"}

0n);\n  const busy = isMining || approving;\n  const canStake = isConnected && !!stakerAddress && parsedAmount > 0n && !insufficientBalance && !busy;\n\n  const setMax = () => setAmount(formatUnits(walletValue, decimals));\n\n  const handleApprove = async () => {\n    if (!usdcAddress || !stakerAddress || parsedAmount === 0n) return;\n    try {\n      setApproving(true);\n      await writeTx(() =>\n        writeErc20({\n          address: usdcAddress,\n          abi: erc20Abi,\n          functionName: \"approve\",\n          args: [stakerAddress, parsedAmount],\n        }),\n      );\n      await refetchAllowance();\n    } catch (e) {\n      console.error(\"Approve failed\", e);\n    } finally {\n      setApproving(false);\n    }\n  };\n\n  const handleStake = async () => {\n    try {\n      await writeStaker({ functionName: \"stake\", args: [parsedAmount] });\n      setAmount(\"\");\n      // Staking consumes the allowance, so refresh it alongside the position.\n      await Promise.all([refetchStaked(), refetchAllowance()]);\n    } catch (e) {\n      console.error(\"Stake failed\", e);\n    }\n  };\n\n  const handleWithdraw = async () => {\n    try {\n      await writeStaker({ functionName: \"withdraw\" });\n      await refetchStaked();\n    } catch (e) {\n      console.error(\"Withdraw failed\", e);\n    }\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"flex flex-col items-center grow w-full px-4 py-10\">\n      <div className=\"w-full max-w-xl flex flex-col gap-6\">\n        <div className=\"text-center\">\n          <h1 className=\"text-4xl font-bold mb-1\">Stake USDC</h1>\n          <p className=\"text-base-content/70\">Deposit USDC and withdraw your full position anytime. No lockups.</p>\n        </div>\n\n        {isConnected && (\n          <div className=\"flex items-center justify-center gap-2 text-sm\">\n            <span className=\"text-base-content/70\">Wallet:</span>\n            <Address address={address} chain={targetNetwork} />\n          </div>\n        )}\n\n        <div className=\"stats stats-vertical sm:stats-horizontal shadow bg-base-100 w-full\">\n          <div className=\"stat\">\n            <div className=\"stat-title\">Wallet {symbol}</div>\n            <div className=\"stat-value text-2xl\">{formatAmount(walletValue, decimals)}</div>\n          </div>\n          <div className=\"stat\">\n            <div className=\"stat-title\">ETH (for gas)</div>\n            <div className=\"stat-value text-2xl\">{formatAmount(ethBalance?.value, ethBalance?.decimals ?? 18, 4)}</div>\n          </div>\n          <div className=\"stat\">\n            <div className=\"stat-title\">Staked {symbol}</div>\n            <div className=\"stat-value text-2xl text-primary\">{formatAmount(stakedValue, decimals)}</div>\n          </div>\n        </div>\n\n        {!isConnected ? (\n          <div className=\"card bg-base-100 shadow-xl\">\n            <div className=\"card-body items-center text-center\">\n              <p>Connect your wallet to stake.</p>\n            </div>\n          </div>\n        ) : !stakerAddress && !stakerInfoLoading ? (\n          <div className=\"card bg-base-100 shadow-xl\">\n            <div className=\"card-body items-center text-center\">\n              <p>Staker contract is not deployed on {targetNetwork.name}.</p>\n            </div>\n          </div>\n        ) : (\n          <>\n            <div className=\"card bg-base-100 shadow-xl\">\n              <div className=\"card-body gap-4\">\n                <h2 className=\"card-title\">Stake</h2>\n                <div className=\"form-control\">\n                  <label className=\"input input-bordered flex items-center gap-2\">\n                    <input\n                      type=\"number\"\n                      min=\"0\"\n                      step=\"any\"\n                      placeholder=\"0.00\"\n                      className=\"grow\"\n                      value={amount}\n                      onChange={e => setAmount(e.target.value)}\n                    />\n                    <span className=\"text-base-content/60\">{symbol}</span>\n                    <button type=\"button\" className=\"btn btn-xs btn-ghost\" onClick={setMax}>\n                      Max\n                    </button>\n                  </label>\n                  {insufficientBalance && <span className=\"text-error text-sm mt-1\">Amount exceeds wallet balance.</span>}\n                </div>\n\n                {needsApproval && parsedAmount > 0n && !insufficientBalance ? (\n                  <button className=\"btn btn-primary\" onClick={handleApprove} disabled={busy}>\n                    {approving && <span className=\"loading loading-spinner loading-sm\" />}\n                    Approve {symbol}\n                  </button>\n                ) : (\n                  <button className=\"btn btn-primary\" onClick={handleStake} disabled={!canStake}>\n                    {isMining && <span className=\"loading loading-spinner loading-sm\" />}\n                    Stake\n                  </button>\n                )}\n              </div>\n            </div>\n\n            <div className=\"card bg-base-100 shadow-xl\">\n              <div className=\"card-body gap-4\">\n                <h2 className=\"card-title\">Your position</h2>\n                <p className=\"text-lg\">\n                  <span className=\"font-bold\">{formatAmount(stakedValue, decimals)}</span> {symbol} staked\n                </p>\n                <button\n                  className=\"btn btn-secondary\"\n                  onClick={handleWithdraw}\n                  disabled={stakedValue === 0n || busy}\n                >\n                  {isMining && <span className=\"loading loading-spinner loading-sm\" />}\n                  Withdraw all\n                </button>\n              </div>\n            </div>\n          </>\n        )}\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n\nexport default Stake;\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}

loadAndExecuteDeploymentsFromFiles } from \"../rocketh/environment.js\";\n\nconst { provider, networkHelpers, ethers } = await network.create();\n\nconst USDC = (n: string) => parseUnits(n, 6);\n\n// Deploy MockUSDC + Staker via the real deploy scripts, then return ethers\n// contract handles wired to distinct signers.\nasync function deployFixture() {\n  const env = await loadAndExecuteDeploymentsFromFiles({ provider });\n\n  const staker = env.get<any>(\"Staker\");\n  const usdc = env.get<any>(\"MockUSDC\");\n\n  const [deployer, alice, bob] = await ethers.getSigners();\n  const stakerC = await ethers.getContractAt(staker.abi, staker.address);\n  const usdcC = await ethers.getContractAt(usdc.abi, usdc.address);\n\n  return { stakerC, usdcC, deployer, alice, bob };\n}\n\ndescribe(\"Staker\", function () {\n  it(\"points at the deployed USDC token\", async function () {\n    const { stakerC, usdcC } = await networkHelpers.loadFixture(deployFixture);\n    expect((await stakerC.usdc()).toLowerCase()).to.equal((await usdcC.getAddress()).toLowerCase());\n  });\n\n  it(\"stakes USDC and tracks the position\", async function () {\n    const { stakerC, usdcC, alice } = await networkHelpers.loadFixture(deployFixture);\n    const stakerAddr = await stakerC.getAddress();\n\n    await usdcC.mint(alice.address, USDC(\"100\"));\n    await usdcC.connect(alice).approve(stakerAddr, USDC(\"100\"));\n\n    await expect(stakerC.connect(alice).stake(USDC(\"40\")))\n      .to.emit(stakerC, \"Staked\")\n      .withArgs(alice.address, USDC(\"40\"));\n\n    expect(await stakerC.stakedBalance(alice.address)).to.equal(USDC(\"40\"));\n    expect(await stakerC.totalStaked()).to.equal(USDC(\"40\"));\n    expect(await usdcC.balanceOf(stakerAddr)).to.equal(USDC(\"40\"));\n    expect(await usdcC.balanceOf(alice.address)).to.equal(USDC(\"60\"));\n  });\n\n  it(\"accumulates multiple stakes\", async function () {\n    const { stakerC, usdcC, alice } = await networkHelpers.loadFixture(deployFixture);\n    const stakerAddr = await stakerC.getAddress();\n\n    await usdcC.mint(alice.address, USDC(\"100\"));\n    await usdcC.connect(alice).approve(stakerAddr, USDC(\"100\"));\n\n    await stakerC.connect(alice).stake(USDC(\"30\"));\n    await stakerC.connect(alice).stake(USDC(\"20\"));\n\n    expect(await stakerC.stakedBalance(alice.address)).to.equal(USDC(\"50\"));\n  });\n\n  it(\"withdraws the full position\", async function () {\n    const { stakerC, usdcC, alice } = await networkHelpers.loadFixture(deployFixture);\n    const stakerAddr = await stakerC.getAddress();\n\n    await usdcC.mint(alice.address, USDC(\"100\"));\n    await usdcC.connect(alice).approve(stakerAddr, USDC(\"100\"));\n    await stakerC.connect(alice).stake(USDC(\"100\"));\n\n    await expect(stakerC.connect(alice).withdraw())\n      .to.emit(stakerC, \"Withdrawn\")\n      .withArgs(alice.address, USDC(\"100\"));\n\n    expect(await stakerC.stakedBalance(alice.address)).to.equal(0n);\n    expect(await stakerC.totalStaked()).to.equal(0n);\n    expect(await usdcC.balanceOf(alice.address)).to.equal(USDC(\"100\"));\n    expect(await usdcC.balanceOf(stakerAddr)).to.equal(0n);\n  });\n\n  it(\"keeps user positions isolated\", async function () {\n    const { stakerC, usdcC, alice, bob } = await networkHelpers.loadFixture(deployFixture);\n    const stakerAddr = await stakerC.getAddress();\n\n    await usdcC.mint(alice.address, USDC(\"100\"));\n    await usdcC.mint(bob.address, USDC(\"100\"));\n    await usdcC.connect(alice).approve(stakerAddr, USDC(\"100\"));\n    await usdcC.connect(bob).approve(stakerAddr, USDC(\"100\"));\n\n    await stakerC.connect(alice).stake(USDC(\"70\"));\n    await stakerC.connect(bob).stake(USDC(\"30\"));\n\n    await stakerC.connect(alice).withdraw();\n\n    expect(await stakerC.stakedBalance(alice.address)).to.equal(0n);\n    expect(await stakerC.stakedBalance(bob.address)).to.equal(USDC(\"30\"));\n    expect(await stakerC.totalStaked()).to.equal(USDC(\"30\"));\n  });\n\n  it(\"reverts when staking zero\", async function () {\n    const { stakerC, alice } = await networkHelpers.loadFixture(deployFixture);\n    await expect(stakerC.connect(alice).stake(0n)).to.be.revertedWithCustomError(stakerC, \"ZeroAmount\");\n  });\n\n  it(\"reverts when withdrawing with nothing staked\", async function () {\n    const { stakerC, alice } = await networkHelpers.loadFixture(deployFixture);\n    await expect(stakerC.connect(alice).withdraw()).to.be.revertedWithCustomError(stakerC, \"NothingStaked\");\n  });\n\n  it(\"reverts staking without allowance\", async function () {\n    const { stakerC, usdcC, alice } = await networkHelpers.loadFixture(deployFixture);\n    await usdcC.mint(alice.address, USDC(\"100\"));\n    // no approve\n    await expect(stakerC.connect(alice).stake(USDC(\"10\"))).to.be.revert(ethers);\n  });\n});\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":1,"oldLines":6,"newStart":1,"newLines":8,"lines":[" import { expect } from \"chai\";"," import { network } from \"hardhat\";"," import { parseUnits } from {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdLXdRE2c3GpsyBNoeG7v","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01WLBdZyJhv4YbcWy9tXMvxu","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-ux-goal-001/2026-07-24T074300Z-claude-no-skill-3/workspace/packages/hardhat/test/Staker.ts","content":"import { expect } from \"chai\";\nimport type { Signer } from \"ethers\";\nimport { network } from \"hardhat\";\nimport { parseUnits } from \"viem\";\nimport type { Abi_MockUSDC } from \"../generated/abis/MockUSDC.js\";\nimport type { Abi_Staker } from \"../generated/abis/Staker.js\";\nimport { loadAndExecuteDeploymentsFromFiles } from \"../rocketh/environment.js\";\n\nconst { provider, networkHelpers, ethers } = await network.create();\n\nconst USDC = (n: string) => parseUnits(n, 6);\n\n// ethers v6 `.connect()` widens back to BaseContract, dropping typed methods;\n// this preserves the original contract's typing.\nconst as = <T extends { connect: (s: Signer) => unknown }>(contract: T, signer: Signer) =>\n  contract.connect(signer) as T;\n\n// Deploy MockUSDC + Staker via the real deploy scripts, then return ethers\n// contract handles alongside a few signers.\nasync function deployFixture() {\n  const env = await loadAndExecuteDeploymentsFromFiles({ provider });\n\n  const staker = env.get<Abi_Staker>(\"Staker\");\n  const usdc = env.get<Abi_MockUSDC>(\"MockUSDC\");\n\n  const [deployer, alice, bob] = await ethers.getSigners();\n  const stakerC = await ethers.getContractAt(staker.abi, staker.address);\n  const usdcC = await ethers.getContractAt(usdc.abi, usdc.address);\n\n  return { stakerC, usdcC, deployer, alice, bob };\n}\n\ndescribe(\"Staker\", function () {\n  it(\"points at the deployed USDC token\", async function () {\n    const { stakerC, usdcC } = await networkHelpers.loadFixture(deployFixture);\n    expect((await stakerC.usdc()).toLowerCase()).to.equal((await usdcC.getAddress()).toLowerCase());\n  });\n\n  it(\"stakes USDC and tracks the position\", async function () {\n    const { stakerC, usdcC, alice } = await networkHelpers.loadFixture(deployFixture);\n    const stakerAddr = await stakerC.getAddress();\n\n    await usdcC.mint(alice.address, USDC(\"100\"));\n    await as(usdcC, alice).approve(stakerAddr, USDC(\"100\"));\n\n    await expect(as(stakerC, alice).stake(USDC(\"40\")))\n      .to.emit(stakerC, \"Staked\")\n      .withArgs(alice.address, USDC(\"40\"));\n\n    expect(await stakerC.stakedBalance(alice.address)).to.equal(USDC(\"40\"));\n    expect(await stakerC.totalStaked()).to.equal(USDC(\"40\"));\n    expect(await usdcC.balanceOf(stakerAddr)).to.equal(USDC(\"40\"));\n    expect(await usdcC.balanceOf(alice.address)).to.equal(USDC(\"60\"));\n  });\n\n  it(\"accumulates multiple stakes\", async function () {\n    const { stakerC, usdcC, alice } = await networkHelpers.loadFixture(deployFixture);\n    const stakerAddr = await stakerC.getAddress();\n\n    await usdcC.mint(alice.address, USDC(\"100\"));\n    await as(usdcC, alice).approve(stakerAddr, USDC(\"100\"));\n\n    await as(stakerC, alice).stake(USDC(\"30\"));\n    await as(stakerC, alice).stake(USDC(\"20\"));\n\n    expect(await stakerC.stakedBalance(alice.address)).to.equal(USDC(\"50\"));\n  });\n\n  it(\"withdraws the full position\", async function () {\n    const { stakerC, usdcC, alice } = await networkHelpers.loadFixture(deployFixture);\n    const stakerAddr = await stakerC.getAddress();\n\n    await usdcC.mint(alice.address, USDC(\"100\"));\n    await as(usdcC, alice).approve(stakerAddr, USDC(\"100\"));\n    await as(stakerC, alice).stake(USDC(\"100\"));\n\n    await expect(as(stakerC, alice).withdraw())\n      .to.emit(stakerC, \"Withdrawn\")\n      .withArgs(alice.address, USDC(\"100\"));\n\n    expect(await stakerC.stakedBalance(alice.address)).to.equal(0n);\n    expect(await stakerC.totalStaked()).to.equal(0n);\n    expect(await usdcC.balanceOf(alice.address)).to.equal(USDC(\"100\"));\n    expect(await usdcC.balanceOf(stakerAddr)).to.equal(0n);\n  });\n\n  it(\"keeps user positions isolated\", async function () {\n    const { stakerC, usdcC, alice, bob } = await networkHelpers.loadFixture(deployFixture);\n    const stakerAddr = await stakerC.getAddress();\n\n    await usdcC.mint(alice.address, USDC(\"100\"));\n    await usdcC.mint(bob.address, USDC(\"100\"));\n    await as(usdcC, alice).approve(stakerAddr, USDC(\"100\"));\n    await as(usdcC, bob).approve(stakerAddr, USDC(\"100\"));\n\n    await as(stakerC, alice).stake(USDC(\"70\"));\n    await as(stakerC, bob).stake(USDC(\"30\"));\n\n    await as(stakerC, alice).withdraw();\n\n    expect(await stakerC.stakedBalance(alice.address)).to.equal(0n);\n    expect(await stakerC.stakedBalance(bob.address)).to.equal(USDC(\"30\"));\n    expect(await stakerC.totalStaked()).to.equal(USDC(\"30\"));\n  });\n\n  it(\"reverts when staking zero\", async function () {\n    const { stakerC, alice } = await networkHelpers.loadFixture(deployFixture);\n    await expect(as(stakerC, alice).stake(0n)).to.be.revertedWithCustomError(stakerC, \"ZeroAmount\");\n  });\n\n  it(\"reverts when withdrawing with nothing staked\", async function () {\n    const { stakerC, alice } = await networkHelpers.loadFixture(deployFixture);\n    await expect(as(stakerC, alice).withdraw()).to.be.revertedWithCustomError(stakerC, \"NothingStaked\");\n  });\n\n  it(\"reverts staking without allowance\", async function () {\n    const { stakerC, usdcC, alice } = await networkHelpers.loadFixture(deployFixture);\n    await usdcC.mint(alice.address, USDC(\"100\"));\n    // no approve\n    await expect(as(stakerC, alice).stake(USDC(\"10\"))).to.be.revert(ethers);\n  });\n});\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":922,"cache_read_input_tokens":115599,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":922},"output_tokens":5,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"e0f8ee0b-4963-49f1-93b4-a491b6632f5f","uuid":"4f88151b-db0c-4945-83a8-bc1f4437538e","timestamp":"2026-07-24T08:00:15.627Z","request_id":"req_011CdLXdPsfbo2VAZaeRfjKj"}
